C. Isomerizes uridine to pseudouridine at positions 54 and 55 in a subset of tRNAs - This matches TruB (pseudouridine synthase B), which modifies U55 to Ψ55. Some variants can also modify U54.

D. Removes introns from precursor tRNAs by cleaving their RNA backbone - This would be tRNA splicing endonuclease, which is a completely different enzyme.

The answer is C. The protein contains the characteristic CTHG catalytic motif of the TruB family of pseudouridine synthases, and the domain architecture (zinc finger + tandem RNA-binding domains + catalytic domain with CTHG) is consistent with TruB, which isomerizes uridine to pseudouridine at position 55 (and in some cases 54) in tRNAs.
